### Windows CI SID identity gate correction

The first c37483a Windows run retained 47 positives / 48 and one failed test:
Windows Server formatted the Administrator account SID as SDDL `LA`, while the
test expected its numeric spelling. The pipe DACL/runtime code is unchanged.
The corrected gate reads the binary ACL and requires protected/non-default DACL,
one non-inheriting allow ACE, exact full file rights, `EqualSid` equality to the
owner token and a non-inheritable pipe handle. Native alias equivalence and
Administrators-group rejection cases prevent string formatting from determining
authorization. All 50 native cases pass on Python3.13 and 3.14.9 with no skips or
source drift. Hosted acceptance of that corrected commit remains required.

Only the native test file changes executable bytes after source 630; all 653 other
frozen executable inputs, including every runtime/Android input, are verified
unchanged. The source 630 build/1004 JVM/359 policy/lint/signing/alignment and
retained-data connected results remain evidence for those unchanged inputs;
this test-only follow-up does not claim a new APK or a new full Android run.
Failed [run 43148625763](https://github.com/DionCroft/Signal_Grove/actions/runs/43148625763)
and artifact 13912787747 are retained with ZIP SHA-256
`06876233beb7addfa318a185b8deecb7e2da6767fd593f791aec9636d9b9fdc4`.
The correction follows Microsoft's [SID string aliases](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/secauthz/sid-strings)
and [EqualSid](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/securitybaseapi/nf-securitybaseapi-equalsid).

## 12 November 2026 - private local worker read transport

An opt-in local Windows pipe now carries the fixed read frames without a public
port, shared secret, persistent queue or content cache. A protected owner-only
DACL and local-client flag restrict access. The client verifies actual broker
PID, creation time, user and session before submission; the server checks the
client token and reverts impersonation before project reads. Actor/profile/root
authorization remains the existing broker's separate responsibility.

Message I/O has finite deadlines and no implicit retry. Failures conservatively
report whether submission may have occurred. Reply hashes are acknowledged;
closure cancels pending pipe I/O, retires leases and discards in-flight contents.

## 8 November 2026 - history feedback and compact error details

The unreleased history reader explains only the documented HTTP 404

## 2026-11-06 — beta.6 conversation-title hotfix preparation

The app now supplies a single-line, Unicode-safe label with a stable 16-hex session-derived suffix. The original message and existing sessions stay unchanged; there is no automatic replay, server rename, model switch or lock clearing. The saved default and verified serving model remain distinct.

The first native attempt remains failed: the new fixture lazily started MockWebServer on the UI thread; the correction moves only fixture URL creation off that thread, preserving every assertion. The application APK is unchanged by that test-only correction.

A separate actual pinned Hermes `b200f5bc60e31588ba2cb60e288029c4a54ba5fa` loopback HTTP fixture reproduced duplicate-title400, accepted two generated labels201, preserved the existing synthetic session and left no rejected new row or model lock. Python audit hooks and isolated environment are not an OS sandbox or a claim about every deployed runtime. Read-only inspection of the deployed handler separately established its title-conflict rollback and model precedence.

All144 source/policy and15 observer tests pass; strict dependency verification remains495 components/1071 artifacts. ## Task navigation after editing — hosted finding

Android CI run [41536487114](https://github.com/DionCroft/Signal_Grove/actions/runs/41536487114) built and verified commit `9cf11c1` strictly. API 26 initially failed before app installation when Google's emulator archive transfer closed; a retry passed the complete **95-case** suite (job 125596737366). API 37 completed 96 cases with one older NavigationTest failure: after editing a task, its completion click raced the leaving IME. That attempt is not reported as green.

The test now clears focus, dismisses and (API 30+) waits for the IME, checks that the one completion checkbox is displayed/off before its pointer click, then asserts both the checked UI and persisted Room value. No production behavior, consent assertion or persistence assertion was removed. A focused API 37 run passed at the hosted Pixel 2 geometry (1080×1920); the emulator was restored afterward. ### Isolated CI emulator disk preflight

The provider-account commit `e915493` passed hosted verify (**126205015892**) and compatibility (**41718591386**). Its [API 26 job 126207721426](https://github.com/DionCroft/Signal_Grove/actions/runs/41718591404) failed before APK installation or instrumentation: emulator **37.2.13** reported **7183 MB available / 7372.8 MB required**. No API 26 test result was produced by that job. The earlier local final suites remain 364/364/365 passing tests. API 37 from that hosted run is a separate checkpoint.

The configured 2048M preference cannot override every first-boot minimum: [Android's emulator source](https://android.googlesource.com/platform/external/qemu/+/refs/heads/emu-32-release/android-qemu2-glue/main.cpp) enforces at least 6 GiB for API 24+ and checks 1.2× capacity. The isolated prebuilt-APK device helper now checks for 8 GiB free after SDK/image installation. Only if needed, it reclaims the SDK's unused NDK installations (present on the [official Ubuntu runner image](https://github.com/actions/runner-images/blob/main/images/ubuntu/Ubuntu2404-Readme.md)); the device job never compiles native code. It validates Linux/GitHub context, the isolated temporary work root, the SDK identity and a nonsymlink direct-child NDK target before the fixed-argument cleanup, then requires sufficient space. No developer SDK, workspace, other toolchain or standing AVD is cleaned. Low capacity without that specific reclaimable tree remains an explicit failure.

ADB readiness now polls the same owned emulator process and serial under the original deadline; an exited process fails promptly with the emulator-log pointer. It does not restart on observation timeouts or rerun instrumentation. Offline regressions cover sufficient capacity/no cleanup, exact NDK-only cleanup and insufficient post-cleanup capacity, developer/wrong-root/missing-target refusal, dead-process detection, transient ADB probe timeouts on the same live process and bounded live readiness. Full hosted device validation of this helper change remains a subsequent checkpoint; no emulator was recreated or wiped on the owner's machine.

Corrected intermediate runs passed **39 API 36** and **12 each API 26/37** before the additional literal body-ID capability/regressions. Final results above include that capability and use final hashes only.

The first complete production run passed **464 API 36** and **465 API 37** cases, while API 26 had one failure in the existing normalized main-model flow: its current write count remained one, but the receipt-count assertion found two. An E-only consistent copy of the stopped fixture database showed one current normalized receipt (2026-11-04 00:18:55 UTC) and an older ordinary fixture receipt (2026-11-03 23:10:48 UTC) under the same scope. The OS reused a mock-server port and the fixture reused a static API key; this was historical fixture contamination, not a duplicated write. `CredentialFixtureRule.uniqueToken` now gives each test a stable private connection namespace. Main-model/custom-endpoint flows and twelve matching management flows use it. No history is deleted and exact mutation/receipt assertions remain; the main-model assertions now identify which count failed. A final test-only strict rebuild and all three final full reruns above passed with the original app bytes.

The unchanged v0.22.6 baseline and its two exact reviewed variants pass the expanded **1,005-handler / 229-field / 57-route / 110-module** watch against stable `b200f5bc60e31588ba2cb60e288029c4a54ba5fa` and main `9302ba9e212c63ba24cdf1f47f26b47bcd3b042e`. New coverage includes provider identity/camel/legacy conversion, merge/key-preservation helpers, generated environment names, exact route bindings and locked main-route sections. These are source/fixture checks, not live Hermes/provider, cleanup/revocation, remote-backend policy or physical/OEM attestation. Probes, broader configuration, the continuing scope/security audit and real interoperability remain active work.

## Kanban task mutation foundation (2026-11-05, beta.5 development)

Implemented the typed data/repository/transport foundation for task create, one-phase text edit, priority, assignment/unassignment, comment, dependency add/remove and deletion. Private immutable values, exact board/default-anchor and full task-detail preflight, canonical project/parent rereads, exact profile/prefix/authentication and shared atomic Room claims preserve existing isolation and one-send uncertainty. ACK is durable before cancellable readback. Comments without a returned ID remain acknowledged, never specifically confirmed. Creation identity, worker exit, file erasure, runtime adoption and path mapping are explicitly unverified. [Source, fields and effects](contracts/2026-11-05-kanban-task-authoring.md).

This is a foundation commit: private forms/view-model integration/localized reviews are the next chunk. No task mutation control is advertised as available yet. Status/overrides/worker/dispatch/auxiliary operations, event/file/pinning/transfer work and the rest of the audit remain open. No backend or fake controls were added.

The first required build failed one new JVM assertion after **8m 31s**: the test incorrectly assumed upstream assignee normalization enforced a filesystem-name grammar. Blob-verified primary `normalize_profile_name` only trims/lowercases. The corrected test asserts exact path-like label data and separately retains the mobile control-character rejection. Product code was unchanged by that correction. The second strict build passed in **4m 29s**, all **498 JVM cases**, and its pair passed **85 focused native cases on each API 26 / 36 / 37**, **255 initial cases**.

Review then identified a meaningful coverage gap: some expected transport bodies came from the same request factory, and rich-create parent/project preflight was only partly covered. Those expectations now use independent literal payloads/UUID checks; two new native methods cover every rich-create field with a real scoped gateway project read and fresh parent read, plus stale parent/project refusal before a claim. No test or assertion was removed. Only three test sources changed; all runtime code stayed identical.

Final required `testDebugUnitTest lint assembleDebug assembleDebugAndroidTest` passed in **2m 41s**, with **498 JVM tests / zero failures, errors or skips**. The original debug signer, package, **106 / 1.1.0-beta.5**, minimum API 26, Signal Grove label and Room 11 remain. No dependency/version/schema/signing change occurred.

- Final debug app SHA-256: `be16e71866c1f4bfe2c133ead6ad73cb71f5481627bc1c9d166b7cb13c63f513` (identical to the initial successful pair).
- Final test APK SHA-256: `3f24cfa6baac930bad706d0d90d26a4bd1dd81a328cb8e40add11d5c55a150a2`.
- Initial successful test APK: `2a19cd3dac546a6430836b5419aa377bda1cc6c2b7bdedd87dd42802a81645a5`; its evidence remains separate.

The final exact pair passed **87 focused native cases per API 26 / 36 / 37**, **261 final cases**, with positive individual markers and no skipped cases. The eight classes cover 19 new task repository/Room cases, 15 board cases, 12 project-write cases, six resource-storage cases, 14 instance-storage cases, history and existing instance/project receipt UI flows. Cases exercise all eight literal routes, canonical project/parent and full private task/history drift, claim races across task/board/project/profile domains, exact-prefix separation, private foreign receipt projection, explicit unknown review, lost replies/post-write errors, cancellation before observation and after ACK, restart recovery and non-identifiable comments. Immutable APK pairs/logs/source hashes and original phone geometry are retained. No uninstall, clear-data, downgrade, reseed, production update or new UI/tablet/live-host claim is made.

All **128 Python checks** pass. The first new synthetic watcher fixture omitted standard baseline keys and failed six cases; using the existing complete baseline helper fixes that fixture with its assertions intact. Both logs remain. The additive watch selects **46** task boundaries, **26** fields and **six** routes, with original graph/profile-normalizer source identities. Every prior fingerprint/variant/absence exception and F0 baseline is retained. Full stable **v0.22.7 / b200f5bc60e31588ba2cb60e288029c4a54ba5fa** and main **6c2bf4b99bf96ed038f0b38b7eea2f38788437af** pass. No upstream Python was executed or broadly rebaselined.

The preceding main-head full Android run **41988459154** was still live when this local chunk completed; no new main push cancels it. Exact-head hosted foundation checks follow a separate branch publication and are reported separately. There is no beta.5 APK/tag/release publication or production-install change. Local/fixture/source evidence does not replace live Hermes, physical-device, OEM/ARM64 and final security/release validation. The full audit goal remains active.

## Measured full-suite CI runtime (2026-11-05)

The exact E2 task-core commit's [Android run 41994724901](https://github.com/DionCroft/Signal_Grove/actions/runs/41994724901) passed verify job **127135037695**. API 26 job **127137812252** completed **807 cases / two failures** in credential entry: `canonicalCustomPoolCanReceiveFreshCredential` and `removeReviewsMutableIndexAndDoesNotClaimRemovedIdentity`, both at the pre-existing entry wait. All **19 new task repository cases** report positive results there. The 805 positive markers and two failure codes were checked against the advertised-digest-verified device archive. This is not a passing full run.

API 37 job **127137812255** timed out at 3600 seconds with **798 positive cases of 808**, no failure/skip codes and no terminal OK marker. The digest-verified archive retains its partial stdout and TestRunner chronology. Instrumentation began at **08:37:17.647 UTC**; case 798 finished at **09:37:08.498**, and case 799 started at **09:37:08.503**, about nine seconds before the deadline. A stalled-case diagnosis is not supported by those timestamps. The run remains failed/incomplete, and no unexecuted case is counted as passed.

Measured suite growth and host variance now have a finite **75-minute full instrumentation / 105-minute device-job** budget, preserving the complete one-shot suite and the same timeout/failure gate. The existing default deadline assertion changes to **4500 seconds**; explicit-shorter-deadline, no-reset/retry, malformed/zero/failed-result and retained-partial-output checks remain. Validation of this maintenance change is the current Python suite; current Android UI validation and new exact-head full CI are separate. This change makes no APK/version/Room/signing/installation changes and does not close the credential-navigation failure by itself.

The existing native login method now additionally clicks the actual editor/sign-in button and completes a second **same-URL, same-actor** login. It requires two exchanges, retains all three separately reviewed MCP mutations, and proves a later stale callback cannot create a third exchange. All prior credential/no-replay assertions remain. ## Reviewed host-task editing controls (2026-11-05, beta.5 development)

## Optional runtime-model observer (6 November 2026)

The versioned companion extension provides a read-only, bearer-authenticated model
observation separately from saved model options and gateway readiness. It accepts
no parameters and uses only the selected profile's existing bounded local-inference
allowlist. Anonymous rejection, authenticated exact model identity and health are
required. Unknown, unsafe and ambiguous observations cannot invent a unique route.
No generation, model loading, routing/default change, credential export or automatic
fallback is performed. The standard Hermes API remains supported without this extension.

**15 Python regressions pass**, including real loopback HTTP, auth-boundary checks,
wrong/multiple identities, redirects, unavailable endpoints, private credentials,
concurrent profile separation and guarded source refusal. Both hosted workflows
now run this suite. The adapter preserves existing source newlines and permissions,
refuses changed source/existing observer files and atomically replaces the API
only after a second hash check. All existing function ASTs except the route table
remain unchanged; the new handler explicitly uses the existing auth decorator.

An owner-approved deployment followed a fresh encrypted backup whose local SHA-256
matched the GitHub asset digest. The final protected-store check found the existing
credential already present and matching, so it was preserved. Earlier standalone
previews lacked its native credential context and are not evidence of a missing
production credential. Only the existing idle gateway was restarted once. No model
or saved default was changed and no model-generation request was submitted.

Live trusted-HTTPS checks returned401 without/with an invalid key,400 for query
parameters,405 for POST and404 for a nonexistent profile. The correct key returned
200, the exact capability, verified serving identity and a distinct configured
default. Existing continuity discovery returned11 read-only ContextGraph tools;
all checked paths resolved. Doctor retained its previous exit1 for optional
unconfigured providers; no repair or cloud credential was added. The post-change
encrypted backup completed success/0; its local encrypted size/SHA-256 match the
private-release asset digest. This verifies a live backup, not an operational
restore. Existing HTTPS and desktop HTTP dashboard auth checks still pass.

This is bounded owner-deployment evidence, not stock-Hermes parity or a release.

head remains held: its full API26 suite passed903 cases, while API37 reported one
Kanban cancellation-control visibility failure among904 cases. Both archives were
